## CHAPTER 14｜The Letter Wall｜情書牆
*英文正文 748 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A college group planned a public wall where people could leave anonymous kind notes. Before anyone called the day strange, Nadia had noticed how quiet the college library could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the smell of ink and tea made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nadia opened the letter notebook and began with what had really happened. Eli did not treat silence as an empty space that needed a clever answer. That made Nadia want to speak in her own words. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “ant” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “hurt,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “sick” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

校內社團計畫設置匿名善意留言牆。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，娜迪亞就注意到，當大家擔心時，大學圖書館會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，墨水與茶的香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。娜迪亞沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開情書筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。艾利不把沉默當成必須用聰明答案填滿的空白。這讓娜迪亞想用自己的話說話。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「ant」，於是停下來詢問意思。「hurt。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「sick」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nadia found this uncomfortable, especially with the honest letter still waiting for an answer. Eli suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. “Let us keep ‘hang’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “shorts” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。娜迪亞覺得不安，尤其誠實的信還等著答案。艾利建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。「把『hang』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「shorts」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Nadia asked them to keep the wall free of names, guesses, and unwanted confessions. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nadia wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Eli asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. The word “clerk” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘several’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

娜迪亞請他們不要在牆上寫人名、猜測或不受歡迎的告白。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。娜迪亞想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。艾利問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。「clerk」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『several』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The group returned to the letter notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nadia asked Eli to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. Nadia circled “chicken” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “room” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘try’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

大家重新看著情書筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。娜迪亞請艾利用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「chicken」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「room」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『try』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Eli helped set up a place where anyone could quietly remove a note about themselves. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nadia did not look away from that part. Eli asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. A little note used “bottle” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “police” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “total” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

艾利協助設置機制，讓任何人都能悄悄取下寫到自己的紙條。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。娜迪亞沒有迴避這一部分。艾利問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。一張小筆記以「bottle」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「police」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「total」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nadia felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nadia closed the letter notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. “bag,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “newspaper” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “size” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。娜迪亞雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。娜迪亞輕輕闔上情書筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。「bag。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「newspaper」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「size」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

## CHAPTER 15｜What June Chose｜瓊的選擇
*英文正文 812 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

June decided to invite her friend to a show in person rather than through a perfect letter. The college library had its own familiar rhythm. Nadia knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nadia looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the letter notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. A little note used “care” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “gift” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “run” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

瓊決定親自邀請朋友看表演，不再追求完美情書。大學圖書館有自己熟悉的節奏。娜迪亞熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。娜迪亞卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。情書筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。一張小筆記以「care」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「gift」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「run」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The first reaction in the college library was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nadia felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Eli pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. Eli did not treat silence as an empty space that needed a clever answer. That made Nadia want to speak in her own words. “above,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “enter” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “pocket” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “wrong” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

大學圖書館裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。娜迪亞兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。艾利指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。艾利不把沉默當成必須用聰明答案填滿的空白。這讓娜迪亞想用自己的話說話。「above。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「enter」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「pocket」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「wrong」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Nadia worried her help was no longer needed, but June thanked her for making the choice easier. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the college library. Nadia took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Eli time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “daughter” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “pleasure,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “too” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

娜迪亞擔心自己不再被需要，瓊卻感謝她讓自己更敢做選擇。麻煩是真實的，即使大學圖書館裡並非每個人都看清了風險。娜迪亞退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓艾利有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「daughter」，於是停下來詢問意思。「pleasure。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「too」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

the writing teacher joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nadia copied the answers into the letter notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. “Let us keep ‘common’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” The morning felt short once everyone began to ask questions. “tennis,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

寫作老師聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？娜迪亞把答案抄進情書筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。「把『common』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」大家開始提問後，早晨顯得很短。「tennis。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

Nadia began to see friendship as something larger than useful words. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nadia could follow. Eli warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nadia agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. The word “choose” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘lovely’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “sleep” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

娜迪亞開始明白，友情比有用的文字更廣大。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成娜迪亞能循著走的一條線。艾利提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。娜迪亞同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。「choose」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『lovely』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sleep」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

When Nadia returned to the college library, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nadia now understood. Eli had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. Nadia circled “chance” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “great” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘season’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

當娜迪亞回到大學圖書館，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是娜迪亞的理解。艾利提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「chance」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「great」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『season』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 15?**
A. The school closes forever without a discussion.
B. June decided to invite her friend to a show in person rather than through a perfect letter.
C. The main character leaves without checking any facts.
**Answer: B**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 15?**
A. Nadia began to see friendship as something larger than useful words.
B. The main character leaves without checking any facts.
C. The school closes forever without a discussion.
**Answer: A**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 16｜A Letter to My Future Self｜寫給未來自己的信
*英文正文 809 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nadia finished the letter to herself and sealed it for the end of the school year. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nadia had been thinking about simple things in the college library when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nadia tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The letter notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. The word “gray” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘page’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “sing” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

娜迪亞寫完給自己的信，封好留待學年結束時再看。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。娜迪亞原本在大學圖書館想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。娜迪亞不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。情書筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。「gray」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『page』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sing」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

For a while, Nadia and Eli stood beside the letter notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nadia was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. Nadia circled “grandfather” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “matter” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘sea’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

娜迪亞與艾利在情書筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。娜迪亞漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「grandfather」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「matter」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『sea』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

She did not write that she must find a boyfriend or win everyone's praise. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nadia chose to admit what was still missing instead. Eli did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nadia wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. A letter belonged to the person who meant it, not the person who could polish it best. Nadia was still learning how to help without taking over. A little note used “bright” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “land” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “report” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

她沒有寫自己一定要交男友或贏得所有人的稱讚。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，娜迪亞卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。艾利沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比娜迪亞原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。信應該屬於真正有心意的人，而非最會修飾字句的人。娜迪亞仍在學習如何幫忙而不越界。一張小筆記以「bright」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「land」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「report」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

The sound of the smell of ink and tea came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nadia stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Eli asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the letter notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. “inside,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “pink” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到墨水與茶的香氣。有一分鐘，娜迪亞不再試著當什麼都懂的人。艾利問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進情書筆記本，並約定誰負責。「inside。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「pink」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

She wrote that she wanted to keep telling the truth, even when it sounded small. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nadia looked from Eli to the letter notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nadia felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “hold” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “pig,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “wonderful” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

她寫下希望自己一直說真話，即使那些話很簡單。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。娜迪亞看看艾利，又看看情書筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。娜迪亞同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「hold」，於是停下來詢問意思。「pig。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「wonderful」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nadia and Eli shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The letter notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. “Let us keep ‘habit’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” The paper was thin, so Nadia kept it flat and dry. “very,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。娜迪亞與艾利交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。情書筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。「把『habit』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」紙張很薄，娜迪亞把它平放並保持乾燥。「very。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 16?**
A. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
B. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
C. Nadia finished the letter to herself and sealed it for the end of the school year.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 16?**
A. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
B. She wrote that she wanted to keep telling the truth, even when it sounded small.
C. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
**Answer: B**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 17｜The Rumor Comes Back｜謠言又回來了
*英文正文 769 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

A student posted a new rumor that Nadia and Eli were secretly together. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nadia both. At the college library, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The letter notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “excited” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “mother,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “throw” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

有學生再度發出流言，說娜迪亞與艾利在祕密交往。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，娜迪亞兩者都遇上了。在大學圖書館，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。情書筆記本讓娜迪亞在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「excited」，於是停下來詢問意思。「mother。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「throw」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

Eli listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nadia to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. “Let us keep ‘drum’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “most” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “test,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

艾利仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。娜迪亞注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。「把『drum』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「most」，於是停下來詢問意思。「test。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

Nadia wanted to write a long answer, but Eli asked whether the rumor deserved their whole day. Nadia felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the honest letter. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Eli drew a line across the letter notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. The word “circle” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘lawyer’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “seed” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

娜迪亞想寫長篇回應，艾利問這則流言是否值得花掉一整天。娜迪亞一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是誠實的信的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。艾利在情書筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。「circle」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『lawyer』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「seed」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

A fresh page in the letter notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nadia wrote down what could happen next, while Eli named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. A letter belonged to the person who meant it, not the person who could polish it best. Nadia was still learning how to help without taking over. Nadia circled “build” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “knowledge” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘remember’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

情書筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。娜迪亞寫下接下來可能發生的事，艾利則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。信應該屬於真正有心意的人，而非最會修飾字句的人。娜迪亞仍在學習如何幫忙而不越界。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「build」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「knowledge」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『remember』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

They made one short correction and returned to their own plans. Once the surprise passed, Nadia wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Eli turned back to the letter notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. A little note used “hand” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “pair” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

兩人只做了簡短澄清，便回到自己的計畫。驚喜過後，娜迪亞開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。艾利重新翻開情書筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。一張小筆記以「hand」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「pair」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nadia remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the letter notebook, Nadia wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. Eli was a friend, not a person who had to agree with every idea. A little note used “old” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “wise” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，娜迪亞記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起情書筆記本前，娜迪亞寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。艾利是朋友，不是必須同意每個主意的人。一張小筆記以「old」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「wise」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

## CHAPTER 18｜The Small Book Fair｜小書展
*英文正文 806 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The writing teacher invited Nadia to share the new workshop rules at a college book fair. Before anyone called the day strange, Nadia had noticed how quiet the college library could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the smell of ink and tea made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nadia opened the letter notebook and began with what had really happened. A little note used “case” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “perhaps” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “turn” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

寫作老師邀娜迪亞在校內書展分享新工坊規則。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，娜迪亞就注意到，當大家擔心時，大學圖書館會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，墨水與茶的香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。娜迪亞沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開情書筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。一張小筆記以「case」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「perhaps」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「turn」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nadia found this uncomfortable, especially with the honest letter still waiting for an answer. Eli suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. “bus,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “move” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “toy” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。娜迪亞覺得不安，尤其誠實的信還等著答案。艾利建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。「bus。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「move」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「toy」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

She feared that talking about mistakes would hurt her old reputation. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nadia wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Eli asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “bread” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “headache,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “tape” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

她擔心談自己的錯誤會傷害過去的名聲。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。娜迪亞想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。艾利問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「bread」，於是停下來詢問意思。「headache。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「tape」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The group returned to the letter notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nadia asked Eli to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. “Let us keep ‘bad’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “excellent” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “something,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

大家重新看著情書筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。娜迪亞請艾利用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。「把『bad』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「excellent」，於是停下來詢問意思。「something。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

Eli reminded her that changing a rule could be a sign of strength. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nadia did not look away from that part. Eli asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. Nadia had learned that a beautiful sentence could be easy to write and hard to mean. Eli never asked her to hide that difference. The word “along” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘dozen’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “sharp” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “zoo,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

艾利提醒她，願意改變規則也是一種勇敢。答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。娜迪亞沒有迴避這一部分。艾利問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。娜迪亞學到，漂亮句子容易寫，真正說到做到卻不容易。艾利從不要求她掩藏差別。「along」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『dozen』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「sharp」，於是停下來詢問意思。「zoo。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nadia felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nadia closed the letter notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. Nadia circled “dinner” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “ruler” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘wake’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。娜迪亞雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。娜迪亞輕輕闔上情書筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「dinner」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「ruler」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『wake』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

## CHAPTER 19｜A Book of Real Voices｜真實聲音之書
*英文正文 752 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

The workshop made a small book of letters written and approved by their own senders. The college library had its own familiar rhythm. Nadia knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nadia looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the letter notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. The word “drink” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘practice’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

工坊製作一本小書，收錄寄件人親自寫下並確認的信。大學圖書館有自己熟悉的節奏。娜迪亞熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。娜迪亞卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。情書筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。「drink」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『practice』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The first reaction in the college library was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nadia felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Eli pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. Nadia circled “cup” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “magic” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

大學圖書館裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。娜迪亞兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。艾利指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「cup」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「magic」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Nadia did not put herself on the cover as the author of everybody's feelings. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the college library. Nadia took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Eli time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. A little note used “cool” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “loud” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “tie” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

娜迪亞沒有把自己放在封面上，假裝所有感情都由她寫出。麻煩是真實的，即使大學圖書館裡並非每個人都看清了風險。娜迪亞退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓艾利有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。一張小筆記以「cool」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「loud」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「tie」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

the writing teacher joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nadia copied the answers into the letter notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. “computer,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. Nadia wanted everyone to reach home safely at the end of the day. Nadia circled “son” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

寫作老師聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？娜迪亞把答案抄進情書筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。「computer。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。娜迪亞希望大家今天都能安全回家。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「son」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Each page carried the name the writer wished to share, or no name at all. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nadia could follow. Eli warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nadia agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “carry” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “food,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “short” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

每頁只放作者願意公開的名字，或完全不署名。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成娜迪亞能循著走的一條線。艾利提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。娜迪亞同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「carry」，於是停下來詢問意思。「food。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「short」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

When Nadia returned to the college library, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nadia now understood. Eli had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. Nadia had learned that a beautiful sentence could be easy to write and hard to mean. Eli never asked her to hide that difference. “Let us keep ‘bed’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “fat” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “school,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

當娜迪亞回到大學圖書館，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是娜迪亞的理解。艾利提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。娜迪亞學到，漂亮句子容易寫，真正說到做到卻不容易。艾利從不要求她掩藏差別。「把『bed』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「fat」，於是停下來詢問意思。「school。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 20｜The Question at the Fair｜書展上的問題
*英文正文 777 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

At the fair, someone asked Nadia whether she had ever written a real love letter of her own. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nadia had been thinking about simple things in the college library when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nadia tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The letter notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. Eli did not treat silence as an empty space that needed a clever answer. That made Nadia want to speak in her own words. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “appear” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “knife,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “soup” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

書展上有人問娜迪亞，是否寫過真正屬於自己的情書。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。娜迪亞原本在大學圖書館想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。娜迪亞不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。情書筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。艾利不把沉默當成必須用聰明答案填滿的空白。這讓娜迪亞想用自己的話說話。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「appear」，於是停下來詢問意思。「knife。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「soup」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

For a while, Nadia and Eli stood beside the letter notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nadia was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. “Let us keep ‘go’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “round” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

娜迪亞與艾利在情書筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。娜迪亞漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。「把『go』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「round」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

She felt her face grow warm and looked at Eli across the room. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nadia chose to admit what was still missing instead. Eli did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nadia wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. The word “fool” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘pool’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

她臉上發熱，望向房間另一邊的艾利。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，娜迪亞卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。艾利沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比娜迪亞原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。「fool」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『pool』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

The sound of the smell of ink and tea came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nadia stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Eli asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the letter notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. Nadia circled “fail” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “mountain” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到墨水與茶的香氣。有一分鐘，娜迪亞不再試著當什麼都懂的人。艾利問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進情書筆記本，並約定誰負責。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「fail」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「mountain」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

She said she was learning, and for once did not add a clever joke. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nadia looked from Eli to the letter notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nadia felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. A little note used “exciting” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “million” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “wet” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

她說自己正在學習，這次沒有再加機智笑話。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。娜迪亞看看艾利，又看看情書筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。娜迪亞同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。一張小筆記以「exciting」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「million」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「wet」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nadia and Eli shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The letter notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. “elephant,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “lazy” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “violin” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。娜迪亞與艾利交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。情書筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。「elephant。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「lazy」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「violin」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

## CHAPTER 21｜The Unwritten Answer｜還沒寫下的答案
*英文正文 730 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Eli confessed that he liked Nadia but wanted no answer born from pressure or public talk. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nadia both. At the college library, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The letter notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. A little note used “club” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “miss” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “truck” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

艾利坦承自己喜歡娜迪亞，但不希望她因壓力或流言作答。有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，娜迪亞兩者都遇上了。在大學圖書館，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。情書筆記本讓娜迪亞在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。一張小筆記以「club」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「miss」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「truck」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

Eli listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nadia to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. Eli did not treat silence as an empty space that needed a clever answer. That made Nadia want to speak in her own words. “always,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “meeting” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “stop” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

艾利仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。娜迪亞注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。艾利不把沉默當成必須用聰明答案填滿的空白。這讓娜迪亞想用自己的話說話。「always。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「meeting」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「stop」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Nadia asked for a few days to understand her own feelings without an audience. Nadia felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the honest letter. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Eli drew a line across the letter notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “kind” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “share,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

娜迪亞請他給自己幾天，不受旁人干擾地想清楚感情。娜迪亞一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是誠實的信的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。艾利在情書筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「kind」，於是停下來詢問意思。「share。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

A fresh page in the letter notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nadia wrote down what could happen next, while Eli named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. “Let us keep ‘holiday’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “national” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

情書筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。娜迪亞寫下接下來可能發生的事，艾利則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。「把『holiday』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「national」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Eli said yes and kept their usual place by the river free for her. Once the surprise passed, Nadia wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Eli turned back to the letter notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. The word “guitar” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘mouse’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

艾利答應，仍為她留著河邊熟悉的位置。驚喜過後，娜迪亞開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。艾利重新翻開情書筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。「guitar」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『mouse』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

Later, when the noise of the day grew quiet, Nadia remembered one face more clearly than any sign or number. The work had not been easy, and the result did not belong to one person. Still, something had moved forward. Before putting away the letter notebook, Nadia wrote one question for the next day. It was not the end of the story yet. Nadia circled “cost” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “month” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘young’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

後來，白天的吵鬧安靜下來時，娜迪亞記得最清楚的是一張臉，而不是任何標誌或數字。工作並不容易，成果也不屬於單一個人。然而，有些事情確實向前走了。收起情書筆記本前，娜迪亞寫下一個留給明天的問題。故事還沒有結束。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「cost」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「month」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『young』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

## CHAPTER 22｜The First Honest Draft｜第一份誠實草稿
*英文正文 800 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nadia wrote one page describing how Eli listened when she had nothing fine to say. Before anyone called the day strange, Nadia had noticed how quiet the college library could become when people were worried. The smallest sounds seemed clear, and the smell of ink and tea made the place feel both familiar and new. Rather than act as if every answer was obvious, Nadia opened the letter notebook and began with what had really happened. The word “dead” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘hungry’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “public” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

娜迪亞寫下一頁，描述艾利如何在她說不出漂亮話時仍願意聽。在人們說這一天奇怪之前，娜迪亞就注意到，當大家擔心時，大學圖書館會變得多麼安靜。細微的聲音變得清楚，墨水與茶的香氣也讓這地方既熟悉又陌生。娜迪亞沒有裝作一切都很明白，而是打開情書筆記本，從真正發生的事情開始。「dead」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『hungry』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「public」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Nobody could say yet how the story would end. Nadia found this uncomfortable, especially with the honest letter still waiting for an answer. Eli suggested they take turns speaking, because repeating the same fear would not create a new solution. A little order returned to the conversation, and that made the next difficult step possible. Nadia circled “clothes” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “god” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘price’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

沒有人知道事情會如何結束。娜迪亞覺得不安，尤其誠實的信還等著答案。艾利建議輪流發言，因為一再重複恐懼並不會帶來新方法。談話恢復了一些秩序，也讓接下來的難題變得有機會處理。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「clothes」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「god」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『price』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

She crossed out the grand words and kept the ordinary moments. This was the point where a simple wish met the world as it really was. Nadia wanted to help, but wanting did not make every road safe or every rule fair. Eli asked what the other people involved might lose if the wrong choice were made. The answer took longer than either had expected. They agreed not to build the rest of their plan on a fact they had never checked. A letter belonged to the person who meant it, not the person who could polish it best. Nadia was still learning how to help without taking over. A little note used “ability” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “glass” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “mathematics” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. They chose to wait for a safe answer rather than act in haste.

**繁中**

她劃掉華麗詞句，留下平凡的時刻。這正是簡單願望遇上真實世界的時刻。娜迪亞想幫忙，但只靠想法不會讓所有路變安全，也不會讓所有規則公平。艾利問，如果選錯了，牽涉其中的人可能失去什麼。兩人花了比預期更長的時間才回答。他們同意，不把整個計畫建立在尚未核實的事實上。信應該屬於真正有心意的人，而非最會修飾字句的人。娜迪亞仍在學習如何幫忙而不越界。一張小筆記以「ability」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「glass」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「mathematics」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。他們選擇等待安全的答案，而不是匆忙行動。

**EN**

The group returned to the letter notebook and looked at the first line again. It now seemed to ask a different question. Nadia asked Eli to explain the idea in plain words, as if telling it to someone who had just arrived. When the idea sounded confusing, they changed the plan rather than blaming the listener. “floor,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “mad” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “thing” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

大家重新看著情書筆記本的第一行，發現它彷彿提出了另一個問題。娜迪亞請艾利用簡單的話解釋，就像對剛來的人說明。說明若讓人困惑，他們就修改計畫，而不是責怪聽的人。「floor。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「mad」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「thing」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

The letter ended with a question about taking another walk together. The answer brought relief, but it also carried a new responsibility. Nadia did not look away from that part. Eli asked who should hear the news next, and whether it belonged to them to share it. They chose the careful answer over the exciting one, though excitement was still in the air. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “engineer” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “lot,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “these” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

信的最後是一個問題：願不願意再一起散步？答案帶來安心，也帶來新的責任。娜迪亞沒有迴避這一部分。艾利問下一個該讓誰知道消息，以及他們是否有權公開。他們選擇審慎的回答，而非最刺激的那個，雖然空氣中仍充滿興奮。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「engineer」，於是停下來詢問意思。「lot。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「these」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The clock moved on. Nadia felt tired, yet there was a good kind of energy left after honest work. Nobody had to say that the day was perfect. They only had to remember what had worked and what still needed care. Nadia closed the letter notebook gently, already curious about the next choice. “Let us keep ‘draw’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “later” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “sit,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

時鐘繼續走。娜迪亞雖然疲倦，卻保有完成誠實工作後的好心情。沒有人必須宣稱這天完美；只要記得哪些方法有效、哪些事仍需照顧。娜迪亞輕輕闔上情書筆記本，開始好奇下一個選擇。「把『draw』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「later」，於是停下來詢問意思。「sit。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

## CHAPTER 23｜A Letter with Her Name｜署上自己名字的信
*英文正文 771 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nadia gave Eli the letter by the river, with her own name written clearly at the bottom. The college library had its own familiar rhythm. Nadia knew the sound of footsteps there, yet something about this moment called for a closer look. It was tempting to make a quick story out of a small surprise. Instead, Nadia looked for one thing that could be checked and one person who deserved to be heard. There was room in the letter notebook for questions as well as answers, and today the questions were beginning to matter. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “cap” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “lesson,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “thick” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

娜迪亞在河邊把信交給艾利，底端清楚寫著自己的名字。大學圖書館有自己熟悉的節奏。娜迪亞熟悉那裡的腳步聲，但此刻某件事值得更仔細地看。一個小驚喜很容易讓人立刻編出解釋。娜迪亞卻先尋找一件可核對的事，以及一位需要被傾聽的人。情書筆記本裡不只可以寫答案，也能記下問題；今天，那些問題開始變得重要。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「cap」，於是停下來詢問意思。「lesson。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「thick」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The first reaction in the college library was a mixture of surprise and hope. Nadia felt both, and did not pretend otherwise. Eli pointed out that a helpful choice for one person might still make trouble for someone else. Together they tried to find a step small enough to test, but useful enough to show whether their idea worked. “Let us keep ‘big’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “kill” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “study,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

大學圖書館裡第一時間既有驚訝，也有希望。娜迪亞兩種感受都有，並不打算假裝沒有。艾利指出，對某人有幫助的選擇，仍可能給另一個人添麻煩。兩人一起尋找一個夠小、能安全測試，同時足以證明想法是否可行的步驟。「把『big』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「kill」，於是停下來詢問意思。「study。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

He read it slowly and asked if she was sure she wanted him to keep it. The trouble was real, even if the danger had not yet become clear to everyone in the college library. Nadia took a step back to let someone with more experience speak. Help did not always mean being the first person to act. That gave Eli time to notice a detail that had been lost in the noise. The word “art” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘important’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “snake” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

他慢慢讀完，問她是否確定願意讓他留著。麻煩是真實的，即使大學圖書館裡並非每個人都看清了風險。娜迪亞退一步，讓更有經驗的人說話。幫忙不一定代表第一個採取行動。這讓艾利有時間注意到先前被吵鬧掩蓋的細節。「art」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『important』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「snake」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

the writing teacher joined the conversation after hearing both sides. The questions were simple, but the answers were not. Who would be affected? What could be checked today? What should wait until someone with the right skill was present? Nadia copied the answers into the letter notebook. A plan became easier to follow when it was written for people besides its maker. A letter belonged to the person who meant it, not the person who could polish it best. Nadia was still learning how to help without taking over. Nadia circled “action” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “hair” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘raise’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

寫作老師聽過雙方意見後加入討論。問題很簡單，答案卻未必簡單。誰會受影響？今天能確認什麼？哪些事要等具備專業能力的人在場？娜迪亞把答案抄進情書筆記本。當計畫是寫給所有人看，而不只是制定者自己時，也就更容易執行。信應該屬於真正有心意的人，而非最會修飾字句的人。娜迪亞仍在學習如何幫忙而不越界。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「action」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「hair」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『raise』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

She nodded and said she wanted to start by being honest friends who liked each other. The new detail made the room seem a little different. Things that had looked unrelated began to form a line Nadia could follow. Eli warned that one clear clue did not mean they knew everything. Nadia agreed and wrote the date beside the note. There was work still to do, but now they understood why that work mattered. A little note used “expect” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “no” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

她點頭，說希望先做彼此喜歡、誠實相待的朋友。新細節讓房間看起來有些不同。原本互不相關的事物，開始連成娜迪亞能循著走的一條線。艾利提醒，一條清楚線索不代表知道全部。娜迪亞同意了，在筆記旁寫下日期。仍有很多事要做，但大家現在知道為什麼這些事重要。一張小筆記以「expect」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「no」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

When Nadia returned to the college library, the place was almost the same. The difference was in what Nadia now understood. Eli had helped without taking away the choice, and that made the help easier to accept. For now, they could rest. Tomorrow would ask its own question. “ear,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “mark” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

當娜迪亞回到大學圖書館，地方看起來幾乎沒變。真正不同的是娜迪亞的理解。艾利提供幫助，卻沒有奪走選擇權，這份幫助也就更容易被接受。現在他們可以休息。明天自有明天的問題。「ear。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「mark」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

## CHAPTER 24｜The Walk After the Letter｜信後的散步
*英文正文 837 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 20 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Eli answered that he liked Nadia too, and they chose to begin dating slowly. A small event can change the meaning of an ordinary day. Nadia had been thinking about simple things in the college library when a detail refused to fit the old picture. For a moment, everyone around seemed busy with something else. Nadia tried not to rush, because a wrong guess could make the problem harder for someone nearby. The letter notebook was close at hand, ready for the facts that would be easy to forget once people began talking. A little note used “foot” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “jacket” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “we” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

艾利回答自己也喜歡娜迪亞，兩人決定慢慢開始交往。一件小事可能改變平凡日子的意義。娜迪亞原本在大學圖書館想著平常的事，卻注意到一個與過去認知不符的細節。那一刻，周圍的人似乎都忙著別的事。娜迪亞不想衝動，因為錯誤猜測可能使身邊的人更為難。情書筆記本就在手邊，可以把眾人開口後容易忘記的事實記下來。一張小筆記以「foot」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「jacket」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「we」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

For a while, Nadia and Eli stood beside the letter notebook and talked in low voices. Neither wanted a loud argument to take the place of useful work. They tried to say exactly what they knew. When they reached a point they could not explain, they wrote a question mark instead of guessing. Nadia was learning that courage could sound like a quiet request for more information. “enjoy,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “island” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “special” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

娜迪亞與艾利在情書筆記本旁低聲交談了一會兒。兩人都不希望吵架取代有用的行動。他們努力說清自己知道什麼。遇到無法解釋的地方，就畫上問號，而不是隨便猜。娜迪亞漸漸明白，勇敢有時只是安靜地要求更多資訊。「enjoy。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「island」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「special」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Neither promised forever or asked the other to give up school, work, or friends. A mistake would have been easier to hide than to explain. Nadia chose to admit what was still missing instead. Eli did not offer praise just to make the room feel better. Instead, there was a useful question about the next step. It was a smaller answer than the one Nadia wanted, but it was an answer they could trust. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “carrot” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “insect,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “since” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

沒有人承諾永遠，也沒有人要求對方放棄課業、工作或朋友。掩蓋錯誤可能比解釋容易，娜迪亞卻選擇承認尚未補齊的部分。艾利沒有為了讓氣氛好些就隨意稱讚，而是提出一個對下一步有用的問題。這個答案比娜迪亞原本想要的小，卻是可以相信的答案。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「carrot」，於是停下來詢問意思。「insect。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「since」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

The sound of the smell of ink and tea came back as everyone paused. For a minute, Nadia stopped trying to be the person with every answer. Eli asked whether their next choice could be undone if it turned out wrong. That question mattered more than a fast promise. They wrote a careful test on the letter notebook and agreed who would be responsible for it. “Let us keep ‘buy’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “hot” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “row,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see.

**繁中**

眾人停下時，又感覺到墨水與茶的香氣。有一分鐘，娜迪亞不再試著當什麼都懂的人。艾利問，如果下一步選錯了，還能不能改正。這問題比匆忙的承諾更重要。兩人把審慎的測試寫進情書筆記本，並約定誰負責。「把『buy』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「hot」，於是停下來詢問意思。「row。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。

**EN**

They walked by the river and talked about the next book they wanted to read. For a heartbeat, nobody spoke. Nadia looked from Eli to the letter notebook, trying to understand how the new fact changed the plan. The answer was not magic that could remove every risk. It was a reason to change one real action before time ran out. Nadia felt nervous and hopeful at once, and decided to keep both feelings in view. Nadia had learned that a beautiful sentence could be easy to write and hard to mean. Eli never asked her to hide that difference. The word “almost” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘hill’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “pin” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

他們沿河散步，聊起下一本想讀的書。有一瞬間，沒有人說話。娜迪亞看看艾利，又看看情書筆記本，試著弄懂新事實如何改變計畫。答案不是能消除所有風險的魔法，而是提醒大家要在時間結束前改變一項真實行動。娜迪亞同時感到緊張與希望，決定坦然面對兩種感受。娜迪亞學到，漂亮句子容易寫，真正說到做到卻不容易。艾利從不要求她掩藏差別。「almost」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『hill』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「pin」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nadia and Eli shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. The next step might be harder, but it would begin with better facts and a little more trust. The letter notebook remained on the table as a quiet reminder that no one had to solve everything alone. Nadia circled “glad” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “medicine” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘zero’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。娜迪亞與艾利交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。下一步也許更難，卻能從較清楚的事實與多一點信任開始。情書筆記本靜靜留在桌上，提醒大家沒有人必須獨自解決所有問題。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「glad」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「medicine」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『zero』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 24?**
A. The main character leaves without checking any facts.
B. Eli answered that he liked Nadia too, and they chose to begin dating slowly.
C. The school closes forever without a discussion.
**Answer: B**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 24?**
A. They walked by the river and talked about the next book they wanted to read.
B. The school closes forever without a discussion.
C. The main character leaves without checking any facts.
**Answer: A**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。


## CHAPTER 25｜A Sentence of My Own｜屬於我的一句話
*英文正文 802 詞；英中對照 6 組；本章新目標詞 21 條*

### 正文與翻譯

**EN**

Nadia opened the writing workshop with a new sign: Your voice comes first. Some days begin with a plan; others begin with a question no one had expected. This one gave Nadia both. At the college library, people were coming and going, each carrying a different idea about what was important. The letter notebook made it possible to hold on to a few clear details while the rest of the world kept moving. The word “doll” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘skirt’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “week” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant.

**繁中**

娜迪亞重新開啟寫作工坊，掛上新牌子：「你的聲音最重要。」有些日子從計畫開始，有些日子卻從意料之外的問題開始。這一天，娜迪亞兩者都遇上了。在大學圖書館，人們來來往往，每個人都覺得不同的事最重要。情書筆記本讓娜迪亞在世界不停變動時，仍能抓住幾個清楚的細節。「doll」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『skirt』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「week」，於是停下來詢問意思。

**EN**

Eli listened without offering an easy answer. That was unusual enough for Nadia to notice. They named the part they understood, then left space for the part that still felt unclear. A good plan did not have to hide its weak points. Someone nearby had a different idea, and the disagreement made the question sharper instead of ending the conversation. Nadia circled “comfortable” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “ship” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation. “Let us keep ‘twice’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.”

**繁中**

艾利仔細聆聽，沒有立刻給出簡單答案。娜迪亞注意到這份不同尋常的耐心。他們先說出已了解的部分，再為仍不清楚的地方留空間。好的計畫不需要掩藏弱點。附近有人提出不同想法，這場分歧沒有讓對話結束，反而讓問題更清楚。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「comfortable」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「ship」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。「把『twice』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」

**EN**

A first-year student asked for a love letter, and Nadia invited her to write the first sentence herself. Nadia felt a quick wish to fix everything at once. Then the faces nearby made the choice more complicated. It was not only about the honest letter. Other people had time, hopes, and worries of their own. Eli drew a line across the letter notebook: facts on one side, guesses on the other. That simple mark changed how they spoke. A little note used “cat” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “pack” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence. The word “trip” appeared in an example someone had written earlier, and Eli asked if the example fit their situation.

**繁中**

一位新生求助情書，娜迪亞請她先自己寫出第一句。娜迪亞一瞬間很想把所有事一次解決，但附近每張臉都讓選擇變得複雜。這不只是誠實的信的問題。其他人也有自己的時間、希望與憂慮。艾利在情書筆記本上畫一條線，一側寫事實，另一側寫猜測。這個簡單記號改變了他們說話的方式。一張小筆記以「cat」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「pack」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。「trip」出現在前人留下的例句裡，艾利問這個例子是否適合現在的情況。

**EN**

A fresh page in the letter notebook gave the group a way to start again without pretending the past had not happened. Nadia wrote down what could happen next, while Eli named the one thing they still needed to ask. They were not making a perfect picture. They were making a map that another person could read and challenge. “allow,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “live” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful. Nadia circled “tidy” on a reading sheet, not to win a test but to remember the meaning of the whole sentence.

**繁中**

情書筆記本的新頁面讓大家重新開始，也不必否認過去發生的事。娜迪亞寫下接下來可能發生的事，艾利則說出仍要詢問的一件事。他們畫的不是完美圖像，而是一張別人也看得懂、也能提出疑問的地圖。「allow。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「live」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。娜迪亞在閱讀單上圈出「tidy」，不是為了考高分，而是為了理解整句話。

**EN**

Later, Eli left Nadia one simple note: I still like talking to you, and she smiled. Once the surprise passed, Nadia wanted to know what the new fact would mean tomorrow. Eli turned back to the letter notebook and crossed out a guess that no longer fitted what they knew. Neither called the mistake foolish. Changing your mind after new evidence was part of learning. In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “afraid” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “interest,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “thank” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

後來艾利留給娜迪亞一張簡單字條：「我還是喜歡和你說話。」她笑了。驚喜過後，娜迪亞開始想知道這個新事實明天會帶來什麼改變。艾利重新翻開情書筆記本，劃掉一個已不符合事實的猜測。兩人都沒有說那是愚蠢的錯。依新證據改變想法，本來就是學習的一部分。在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「afraid」，於是停下來詢問意思。「interest。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「thank」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

**EN**

There was no great speech at the end of the day. Nadia and Eli shared a look that said they had both noticed the change. Nadia put away her pen with a quiet smile. She still loved good sentences, but now she knew a feeling could belong to her even if its words were plain. Eli waited outside the workshop, and the next conversation would begin without a script. “Let us keep ‘able’ in our word notes,” Nadia said. “We can check its meaning again if we need it.” In the corner of the letter notebook, Nadia found the word “goodbye” beside an old drawing and stopped to ask what it meant. “sometimes,” Eli read from the practice page, then tried to connect the word to something they could actually see. A little note used “word” as its key word; Nadia kept the note because the rest of the line was still useful.

**繁中**

這一天結束時沒有華麗演說。娜迪亞與艾利交換眼神，知道彼此都看見了改變。娜迪亞帶著安靜的微笑收好筆。她仍喜歡漂亮句子，如今卻知道即使話語普通，感情也可以真正屬於自己。艾利在工坊外等待，下一段對話不需要劇本。「把『able』留在詞彙筆記裡吧，」娜迪亞說，「有需要時再確認意思。」在情書筆記本的角落，娜迪亞看見一幅舊畫旁寫著「goodbye」，於是停下來詢問意思。「sometimes。」艾利讀出練習頁上的字，接著試著把它和眼前真正看得到的東西連起來。一張小筆記以「word」為關鍵字；娜迪亞留下它，因為剩下的句子仍然有用。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. What happens near the beginning of Chapter 25?**
A. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
B. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
C. Nadia opened the writing workshop with a new sign: Your voice comes first.
**Answer: C**｜正確選項描述本章開頭實際發生的事件。

**Q2. What new fact or choice appears near the end of Chapter 25?**
A. They throw away every note and forget the problem.
B. Later, Eli left Nadia one simple note: I still like talking to you, and she smiled.
C. A stranger tells them that no further choice is possible.
**Answer: B**｜正確選項對應本章結尾已明確寫出的發展。
